husky - DEPRECATED

Please remove the following two lines from .husky/pre-push:

#!/usr/bin/env sh
. "$(dirname -- "$0")/_/husky.sh"

They WILL FAIL in v10.0.0

Running pre-push: staged checks + tests

> cascade-monorepo@1.0.0 sb:hook:pre-commit
> npm run sb:hook:secrets-gate && node .simplebeacon/qa/pre-commit-gate.cjs


> cascade-monorepo@1.0.0 sb:hook:secrets-gate
> node packages/simplebeacon-cli/bin/simplebeacon.js secrets-gate --path .

No secrets detected in staged files
[pre-commit-gate] No staged scannable files. Skipping gate scan.
Running quick test suite (if present)

> simplebeacon@1.1.2 test
> node --test --test-concurrency=1 --test-timeout=120000 "tests/**/*.test.js"
